- **paper-translation**: Use when translating academic papers (PDF to Chinese Markdown), extracting PDFs with MinerU API, or needing to preserve LaTeX formulas/images/tables through a translation workflow ([APLaS-Plus/paper-translation](https://github.com/APLaS-Plus/paper-translation/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-translation) · ★3)
- **paper-translation**: 학술 논문을 EN/KR 문단 병렬 형식의 한국어 전문 번역(`translation.md`)으로 생성한다. 원문 문단과 한국어 번역을 교차 배치해 대조 가능하게 만든다. "번역해줘", "전문 번역", "한글 번역본", "번역 범위 늘려/줄여" 요청 시 사용. ([chpark-ML/research-review](https://github.com/chpark-ML/research-review/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-translation))
- **paper-translator**: Use when the user asks to translate an academic paper, PDF, Word/.docx manuscript, journal article, or foreign-language literature (e.g. "翻译这篇文献", "把PDF翻译成中文", "translate this paper", "把期刊全文译为中文", "摘要翻成中文", 中译某篇论文, 上传英文文献或 Word 论文要求翻译), including scanned PDFs and papers whose figures sit on separate pages. ([obenic/paper-translator](https://github.com/obenic/paper-translator/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **paper-translator**: Translate English research papers (PDF) into Japanese while preserving the original figure and table layout. Triggered when the user provides a PDF path and asks to translate it, or says things like "この論文翻訳して" / "論文を日本語訳して" / "translate this paper". ([ryotaro0213/paper-translator](https://github.com/ryotaro0213/paper-translator/tree/HEAD/plugins/paper-translator/skills/paper-translator))

- **paper-triage**: Triage an ambiguous paper query into likely interpretations, a ranked shortlist, and recommended follow-up workflows ([zongmin-yu/semantic-scholar-skills](https://github.com/zongmin-yu/semantic-scholar-skills/tree/HEAD/skills-src/paper-triage) · ★21 · 2 copies)
- **paper-triage**: For each discovered paper, decide whether the abstract/TLDR/Consensus-snippets are enough, or whether the full text is needed. LLM-judgment per paper. Gates `paper-acquire` — no PDF may be fetched without a "needs full text" verdict here. ([epireve/coscientist](https://github.com/epireve/coscientist/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-triage) · 2 copies)
- **paper-triage**: Rapidly triage a research paper and produce a SKIM/READ/DEEP/SKIP verdict. Use when the user wants a quick read-or-skip decision on an arxiv, Zotero, DOI, or PDF paper, or is prioritizing from a reading queue. Produces a distilled central claim, method category, required background, and a decision with rationale. Does not write to the vault unless the user asks. ([rusen-archive/rusen-agent-skills](https://github.com/rusen-archive/rusen-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-triage))
- **paper-triage**: 논문 관련도 선별. raw.md의 abstract를 주제 대비 0-5로 Claude가 직접 점수화(LLM-native, 외부 API 없음). 점수는 runtime only — raw.md frontmatter 미터치, 매 실행 독립. summarizer 입력 큐를 추려 Gemini digest 낭비를 줄인다. 부수 효과로 research/topics/&lt;slug&gt;.md에 토픽 이력 로그 append. 트리거 '논문 선별', '관련도 triage', 'relevance filter', 'abstract 점수화'. ([youai058/research_hub](https://github.com/youai058/research_hub/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-triage))
- **paper-triage-skill**: Use when a user provides paper PDFs, paper text, titles, abstracts, DOI lists, candidate reading lists, or a research direction and needs a first-pass literature triage workflow. This skill turns a pile of candidate papers into an actionable reading queue, classifies papers as Deep read, Skim, Park, or Skip, recommends where to start reading, and lists source-verification items. Use for graduate reading, group meeting preparation, thesis opening, literature review scouting, method search, Obsidian note preparation, and handoff to deeper paper reading. Do not use it to fabricate literature rev… ([Zero-stargazer/paper-triage-skill](https://github.com/Zero-stargazer/paper-triage-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-triage-skill))
- **paper-triggered-survey**: Paper-triggered literature survey and idea generation. Given a PDF, arXiv URL, or tweet containing a paper reference, analyzes the paper, searches for related work (including papers the input may have missed), performs cross-domain exploration, and proposes 2-3 innovation directions. Use when the user provides a specific paper and wants to understand its landscape, find extensions, or generate research ideas. Also triggers for "survey this paper", "what's related to this", "extend this work". ([JeanDiable/academic-research-plugin](https://github.com/JeanDiable/academic-research-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-triggered-survey) · ★25)
- **paper-tutorial-briefing**: Turn an AI paper into a rigorous but readable tutorial briefing. The goal is not to paraphrase the abstract; the goal is to help a researcher understand what the paper changes, what evidence supports it, and what should be tried locally next. ([StaryMoon/ai-researcher-skills](https://github.com/StaryMoon/ai-researcher-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-tutorial-briefing))
- **paper-tweet-generator**: Generates a structured reading tweet from an academic paper (PDF, Word, or Text), highlighting specific product advantages. Use when the user wants to turn a document into a social media post or reading summary. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Other/paper-tweet-generator) · ★1.9k)
- **paper-types**: Use when selecting or learning about paper types. Given research questions or a study design, identifies the matching paper type (11 types: IMRaD empirical, systematic/meta-analysis, narrative review, scoping review, qualitative, case report, RCT report, observational, theoretical, CS conference, short paper) and points to its reference guide and rubric. ([mtvrkan/scholar-kit](https://github.com/mtvrkan/scholar-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-types))
- **paper-using-skills**: 《Claude Code 科研手记》论文写作纪律 skill 集的入口与强制触发器。 建立"1% 可能适用就必须调用"的硬规则，列出本系列各核心纪律 skill 的触发条件， 防止用户在赶论文 / 紧张 / 熟练之后跳过该有的检查动作。 Use when 开始任何科研写作场景：写论文、改论文、整理参考文献、 画图、整理导师反馈、跑批量任务、改 Word、提交前检查 等。 ([Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills](https://github.com/Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills/tree/HEAD/paper-using-skills) · ★19)
- **paper-validator**: This skill should be used when conducting comprehensive reviews of research papers for top-tier computer science conferences. Use for identifying weaknesses, unclear statements, missing evidence, and structural issues in academic papers. The skill autonomously reads files, searches for patterns, analyzes structure, and provides constructive peer-reviewer feedback. ([minhuw/claude-writer](https://github.com/minhuw/claude-writer/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-validator) · ★10)
- **paper-verification**: Use when the user wants to verify paper claims against code or data, audit numerical accuracy, check formula-code alignment, or validate citation accuracy. Triggers on phrases like "verify claims", "check numbers", "do the numbers match", "formula vs code", "audit the paper", or "cross-check results". ([fcakyon/phd-skills](https://github.com/fcakyon/phd-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/paper-verification) · ★403 · 3 copies)

- **paper-verify-before-handoff**: 在把任何成果交付给用户（"改完了"、"已完成"、"可以发给导师"、"可以提交"）之前， 必须运行一份硬检查清单：术语一致性、引用完整性、数据正确性、图表编号、 内部交叉引用、字数 / 格式合规、未完成 todo 残留、AIGC pattern（涉及文段改写时跑 chinese-de-aigc 五维自评）、 论证一致性（涉及核心声明改动时跑 paper-logical-consistency 复核）、 改动概要——任意一项 fail 都不能宣告完成。 Use when 你即将说「改完了 / 完成了 / 可以提交了 / 可以发给导师了」、 或用户问「是不是可以了」「还有什么要检查的吗」。 ([Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills](https://github.com/Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills/tree/HEAD/paper-verify-before-handoff) · ★19)

- **paper-workflow**: 将社科实证研究从 idea、proposal、数据或已有结果推进到可复现分析和完整论文 DOCX。 支持研究设计、数据审计、估计、表图、写作与修订，以及按需准备投稿包；按证据完成阶段， 不承诺显著结果或录用。触发：/paper-workflow、从想法到论文、从数据到 docx、推进已有实证稿件。 ([brycewang-stanford/Paper-WorkFlow](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Paper-WorkFlow/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★34)
- **paper-workflow**: 经管 / 社科实证论文全流程 meta-orchestrator：把选题、设计、数据、估计、方法闸门、 表图、写作、去 AI 味、质量门、修订、投稿与复盘编排成 Stage 0–9 可断点续跑流水线。 本 skill 不重复实现子能力，而是按阶段调用既有 skill 或并行 subagent，并在 Stage 3–4 路由 Python/StatsPAI、Stata、R 三种分析后端。触发：/paper-workflow、帮我写一篇实证论文、 从选题到投稿、端到端 empirical paper、已有 proposal/数据/初稿要推进到投稿，或明确要求 用 Stata / R/fixest / Python-StatsPAI 完整复现。 ([caixinyu2017-star/xixi](https://github.com/caixinyu2017-star/xixi/tree/HEAD/plugins/auto-empirical-research-skills/skills/69-Paper-WorkFlow) · ★2)
